- **superposition**: Use when reasoning about superposition — the state before measurement is a weighted combination of all seven horo eigenstates at once (|ψ⟩ = Σ cₙ|n⟩), with Σ|cₙ|² = 1 (the Born rule), which is double-entry balance closing at unity. It is accept-always then normalise-into-harmony; measurement is the collapse that picks one eigenstate with probability |cₙ|² — the leap to a definite level. A basis state collapses to itself.
